description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.

description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.

--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 7469
entity_slug: bay-breeze-rv-on-the-bay
google_review_name: places/ChIJvWiOTkwbmogRf4s5B5YRVGU/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT25KWlZ6ZGxNVGxIV25CWVVqbEliMVF6VFc0NFQyYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: We have been coming here a few times over the past few years. Started with the original owners and had a tow behind. New to RVing they were very helpful guiding us while we backed in.
The new owners are amazing also. They are a family committed to working together. Tammy and one of her two sons Patrick greeted us and helped guide us into position with our 32 foot class A. The host family are very hospitable.
We have stayed in both water front sites #10 & 11 also #9. I don’t think there is a bad site here. We enjoyed the fall season and the breezes.
The park is small , 25 spaces, which is one of the reasons we LOVE it here. However they also have several cottages available to rent. A great option for non RVers to be able to enjoy an intimate setting on the Bay.

description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.

item_id: f3527332-bbc3-4355-9935-885f76b27cf6
name: Bay Breeze RV on the Bay
description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.

--- entity_offer [row 1 of 1] ---
offer_id: 13291
entity_slug: bay-breeze-rv-on-the-bay
name: Bay Breeze RV on the Bay
description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.
offer_type: room
sort_order: 0
is_available: true
legacy_source: bookable_resources
legacy_id: e790f71a-dbea-4da7-b5ab-a70e1cf28470

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 4b0cfd0f-212d-4e3f-abce-3e9f6546a2be
entity_slug: bay-breeze-rv-on-the-bay
reviewer_name: Nancy Bowman
rating: 5
body: We have been coming here a few times over the past few years. Started with the original owners and had a tow behind. New to RVing they were very helpful guiding us while we backed in.
The new owners are amazing also. They are a family committed to working together. Tammy and one of her two sons Patrick greeted us and helped guide us into position with our 32 foot class A. The host family are very hospitable.
We have stayed in both water front sites #10 & 11 also #9. I don’t think there is a bad site here. We enjoyed the fall season and the breezes.
The park is small , 25 spaces, which is one of the reasons we LOVE it here. However they also have several cottages available to rent. A great option for non RVers to be able to enjoy an intimate setting on the Bay.

Cabins are cute from the outside, didn't see inside any so can't rate.

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: 6f5ce535-05ce-454e-a5b2-24521a4cb394
entity_slug: bay-breeze-rv-on-the-bay
reviewer_name: Cathy Vandiver
rating: 5
body: Do yourself a favor,  reserve your spot before you miss out. Just like the sign at the pier, another day at Paradise,  we stayed almost two whole weeks. Every time we took an outing and returned,  the breathtaking view of the bay made us wish we could stay here forever.  No matter how hot it was we had shade and a Breeze, just like the name, Bay Breeze !  Tammy was the most bubbly,  cheerful host you could imagine and Patrick was friendly and professional,  able to help with set up and any thing  needed.  Pet Friendly is an understatement,  every time Tammy or Patrick came by they were petting and treating our dogs, so pets are Priority here for sure. Even a convenient dog walk area with clean up bags is provided. The nature sights and sounds made our camping experience outstanding,  Dolphins,  Osprey and Eagles were a daily visit. Don't know what else to say about this GEM, but please don't pass this by. You deserve to see this creation on your next vacation ❤️

--- entity_section_items [row 1 of 1] ---
id: f3527332-bbc3-4355-9935-885f76b27cf6
section_id: b96d7482-1e3e-4b00-93e7-2d8ed0235a74
entity_slug: bay-breeze-rv-on-the-bay
item_name: Bay Breeze RV on the Bay
description: Welcome to Bay Breeze RV on the Bay, one of Alabama's best kept secrets! Conveniently located on the Bon Secour Bay across from the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, we are a pet friendly park comprised of 25 RV sites (Full-hookup) and 5 Rental Houses with a 150' fishing pier, natural beach, and the best sunrises and sunsets imaginable. The Fort Morgan Peninsula offers a unique mix of bay and beach life that is sure to have something for everyone. From here you can take the scenic drive west passing through the Bon Secour Wildlife Refuge, beautiful beaches and historical Fort Morgan on your way to the Dauphin Island Ferry for a day adventure. Or if you want to enjoy the beauty of Gulf Shores and Orange Beach they are a short drive east and boast the world-renowned Gulf State Park which is a must for any avid cyclers, runners, or wildlife enthusiasts.
icon: 🏠

sort_order: 1

check_out_time: 11:00
